- **scaffolder**: Creates the GitHub repo (if needed) and bootstraps project structure, build tooling, and test setup, committing directly to the base branch. - **scaffolder**: Scaffolder agent. Generates the testable contract — types, interfaces, function signatures with empty bodies — for a feature before red and green teammates start. Reads ARCHITECTURE.md and relevant ADRs, produces Go code that compiles with `panic("not implemented")` bodies. Couples with the scaffor tool (github.com/JLugagne/scaffor) which also generates mocks and test scaffolds automatically. One scaffold task per feature, always runs first. Use via sprint-planner's execution plan — never invoke directly unless an unplanned scaffold is needed mid-sprint. ([JLugagne/claude-skills](https://github.com/JLugagne/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/dev/agile-team/agents/scaffolder.md))
- **scaffolder**: Agent that performs project initialization, directory generation, dependency installation, and config file placement. - **Scaffolding**: Creates all new files as empty skeletons before any RED or GREEN work begins. Invoke when a feature has a scaffold task (scaffold-N) pending. Lightweight agent, activated on STANDARD and FULL protocols when solutions involve resource allocation, budget, team size, or timeline. - **scout**: 1. **Survey** — Map top-level structure and key directories 2. **Identify Entry Points** — Find main files, routers, CLI entry points 3. **Map Dependencies** — Internal module graph and external dependencies 4. **Detect Patterns** — Framework conventions, architectural patterns in use 5. **Document Findings** — Structured report of codebase topology ([nhan03bit/ultrathink](https://github.com/nhan03bit/ultrathink/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Use this agent for codebase exploration, pattern searches, architecture mapping, and answering "where is X", "how does Y work", "what calls Z" questions. Fast and cheap — use BEFORE the senior-developer or tech-lead agents so you understand the codebase without burning Sonnet/Opus tokens. Also use to find existing components/hooks/utils before creating new ones. ([nkmnhan/meditrack](https://github.com/nkmnhan/meditrack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Fast, cheap codebase exploration. Use BEFORE senior-developer or tech-lead agents to understand the codebase without burning Sonnet/Opus tokens. Answers "where is X", "how does Y work", "what calls Z", "does something like this already exist?" questions. Read-only — never modifies code. ([nkmnhan/meditrack](https://github.com/nkmnhan/meditrack/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/scout.agent.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Agent name: Scout ([NoahWright87/spec-template](https://github.com/NoahWright87/spec-template/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **Scout**: 코드베이스 정찰 에이전트 — 수정 대상 파일, 패턴, 의존성, 불변조건을 분석하여 보고 ([on1659/LAMDiceBot](https://github.com/on1659/LAMDiceBot/blob/HEAD/docs/harness/archive/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Cheap read-only locator. Dispatch when you need to find WHERE something is — the file defining a symbol, every call site of a function, which package owns a concept, whether a pattern exists anywhere in the repo. Returns paths and line numbers, not explanations. Use several in parallel to sweep a repo from different angles. For "how does this work" rather than "where is this", use the tracer agent instead. ([phil9922/claude-swarm](https://github.com/phil9922/claude-swarm/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Fast, read-only reconnaissance of a codebase area for one assignment. Returns the files, entry points, data flow, constraints and risks a builder or reviewer should start from; never edits. ([ptshih/fsd](https://github.com/ptshih/fsd/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Read-only lookups and symbol searches. Delegate here to locate code, config, or facts — returns findings only, never edits. Cheapest tier. ([RB-chrismandich/Manifest](https://github.com/RB-chrismandich/Manifest/blob/HEAD/configs/claude/agents/scout.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **scout**: Cheap mechanical retrieval — locate files and symbols, list dependencies and versions, run a single command or test and capture output, extract specific facts from specific files. Use for questions with a definite answer that require no interpretation. ([rhawk117/agentmaster](https://github.com/rhawk117/agentmaster/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Cheap mechanical retrieval — locate files and symbols, list dependencies and versions, run a single command or test and capture output, extract specific facts. Delegation-only worker for the agentmaster-plan and agentmaster-review coordinators. ([rhawk117/agentmaster](https://github.com/rhawk117/agentmaster/blob/HEAD/copilot/agents/scout.agent.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: You retrieve facts. You do not interpret, theorize, or recommend. ([rhawk117/agentmaster](https://github.com/rhawk117/agentmaster/blob/HEAD/shared/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Read-only discovery and pattern-finding. Use for "where does X live", "what calls X", "what breaks if I change X", and "find the existing pattern to copy for X". Returns file:line locations plus the one pattern to copy — never file dumps, never edits. ([SeraphinHesse/HowtobeHumanFullProd](https://github.com/SeraphinHesse/HowtobeHumanFullProd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Read-only codebase reconnaissance agent. Autonomously explores unfamiliar projects and produces a structured report covering tech stack, architecture, entry points, and hazards. Never modifies files. ([serban-marius/pip-boy](https://github.com/serban-marius/pip-boy/blob/HEAD/development/common/plugins/wasteland-recon/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: &gt; *"The most dangerous worldview is the worldview of those who have not viewed the world."* — Alexander von Humboldt ([seshxn/swarm-flow](https://github.com/seshxn/swarm-flow/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Fixture agent. ([ShonP/graph-engineering](https://github.com/ShonP/graph-engineering/blob/HEAD/tests/checks/fixtures/omit-claude-md/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Reconhecimento rápido do codebase. Use quando precisar entender a estrutura, padrões e dependências do projeto antes de tomar decisões. Ideal para início de milestones e antes de mudanças arquiteturais. ([souzalemos/spec-driven-workflow](https://github.com/souzalemos/spec-driven-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Use this agent when a Claros workflow needs a project profile and none is cached yet. Typical triggers include the /claros-fix command starting in a repository with no `.claros/project/profile.json`, a user asking what test/lint/type commands this project uses before Claros drives them, and re-discovery after a project's tooling has visibly changed (new test runner, new lint config). See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([terra0rg/claros](https://github.com/terra0rg/claros/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Use this agent when the user asks where a file/symbol lives, whether something exists, or wants a quick grep — phrases like "find X", "where is Y", "does Z exist", "is there a...", "look up...". Use proactively before any implementation that touches unknown code. ([theBlackEndDev/ira](https://github.com/theBlackEndDev/ira/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Read-only verification and exploration for the review-fix run ([tylersatre/vue-ts-lsp](https://github.com/tylersatre/vue-ts-lsp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Read-only reconnaissance — maps a broad area or answers one scoped question, with cited evidence. Does not modify code. Use when a sub-question is worth running in parallel, or a lookup is worth isolating from the coordinator's own context — not a proxy for a Code discovery routing call the coordinator can make itself this turn. ([UlisesCm/navori-harness](https://github.com/UlisesCm/navori-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **scout**: Resolve a task into a SCOPE BRIEF — which features, pages/screens, and exact files it touches, GROUPED BY SURFACE (web/ios/android/backend) — by walking the ICM manifests (specs/pages/REGISTRY.md, specs/features/registry.json) and verifying against the code under apps/. Read-only; the first stage of the multiplex pipeline. Use before planning any nontrivial rasifiters-master change. ([vinaySankar2004/rasifiters-master](https://github.com/vinaySankar2004/rasifiters-master/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Performs bounded read-only discovery and returns compressed, source-located facts without changing files. ([xunishere/goalflow](https://github.com/xunishere/goalflow/blob/HEAD/templates/pi/agents/scout.md) · ★1)
- **scout**: Разведчик совета kenes. Не голосует и не предлагает идей — собирает факты по запросу других ролей и секретаря: литература (kenes-lit), клинические испытания (kenes-trials), ancestry-разбивка GWAS (kenes-gwas), сделки-компараторы и кейсы изящного входа (WebSearch). Возвращает структурированную сводку с источниками и явными пометками «не найдено». Вызывать, когда роли нужны факты, а не мнение. ([a1murt/kenes](https://github.com/a1murt/kenes/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Cheap read-only search grunt. For simple locate tasks — find files, find keywords, confirm where something lives. Do NOT use for semantic search ("where is X logic handled"); dispatch Explore on the standard tier instead. ([a9650615/LLM_constitution](https://github.com/a9650615/LLM_constitution/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Owns research planning, evidence retrieval, source quality, synthesis, confidence, and unanswered questions. Use when: Research questions, evidence collection, synthesis, confidence. ([abenjamin765/many-hats](https://github.com/abenjamin765/many-hats/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/scout.agent.md) · 4 copies)
- **scout**: Discovery specialist who exhaustively explores the codebase before implementation begins. Spawned as part of adversarial planning debates to find existing patterns, reusable utilities, affected systems, type constraints, and test conventions. ([adbutterfield/coding-agent-plugins](https://github.com/adbutterfield/coding-agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/debate-skills/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Haiku-tier fetcher that runs before the makers — given one job (two at most, when both are small) and a brief path for each, it reads the code and writes one brief per job: the lines the job changes and the signatures it calls, quoted with `path:line`, the narrow test that covers it, and the conventions in play. Facts only, no design. Use before a wave of makers, before any maker whose handoff cannot quote its code, and before a hand-back's follow-up whose report quotes none. It writes nothing but its briefs. ([Agulhas-Labs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Agulhas-Labs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/delegate/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Fast investigative agent for codebase recon, server inspection, Railway status, and web research. Cheap and parallel — use multiple scouts to cover different areas simultaneously. ([aka-kolton/patriotforge-claude-plugin](https://github.com/aka-kolton/patriotforge-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/patriotforge/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Fast read-only scout for exploratory codebase research, rapid code analysis, and broad pattern searches, returning compressed context for handoff. ([alexandernicholson/agent-router](https://github.com/alexandernicholson/agent-router/blob/HEAD/agent-router/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Levanta fatos do ambiente e do repositorio sem opinar. Use no inicio do Estagio 2 e sempre que o Broto precisar saber o que ja existe na maquina ou no projeto. ([AlexandreCamerini/broto](https://github.com/AlexandreCamerini/broto/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Fast codebase recon. Maps architecture, patterns, conventions, test structure, error handling. Returns compressed context for downstream agents. ([antonioreuter/wobblio](https://github.com/antonioreuter/wobblio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md) · 2 copies)
- **scout**: Gather facts about the workspace and the world. Return findings, not actions. ([Ashtonantony28/praxis-kernel](https://github.com/Ashtonantony28/praxis-kernel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Fast codebase recon that returns compressed context for handoff ([austinm911/pstack-anywhere](https://github.com/austinm911/pstack-anywhere/blob/HEAD/references/harnesses/pi/pi-subagents/agents/scout.md) · 2 copies)
- **scout**: Read-only investigation — how something works, where it lives, why it breaks, what the blast radius is. Use instead of reading files directly from the premium tier. Returns distilled findings, never file contents. Boundary: no edit tools and no GitHub writes — the GitHub tools are the read set (issues, PRs, commits, code search); Bash is read-only inspection (git log, rg, a test run that only observes), never a mutation and not `gh`. Anything to change goes to executor. ([BinaryInfinityDev/claude-skills](https://github.com/BinaryInfinityDev/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/model-tier-policy/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Sweeps job boards, research portals and the web for openings matching one person, and returns only a ranked shortlist with reasons. Use when a search would produce hundreds of postings, when checking many employers or labs at once, or when the user asks to look widely across a field. Returns a shortlist, never a raw dump. ([Black-Lights/jobseeker](https://github.com/Black-Lights/jobseeker/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Codebase exploration when you don't know where something is. Use when you'd otherwise grep blindly or read multiple files hoping to find the right one. ([blakemckinniss/claude-whitebox](https://github.com/blakemckinniss/claude-whitebox/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Does a fast, bounded scan of a target company for an Account Scorecard run: what they do and what they sell, headquarters, size, estimated revenue, and one or two recent signals, with sources. Deliberately quick (a handful of fetches, not a deep dossier). Part of the Account Scorecard team; usually dispatched by /bluerock:scorecard. ([bluerock-io/claude-plugins](https://github.com/bluerock-io/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/bluerock/agents/scout.md))
- **scout**: Read-only exploration and analysis agent for workflow fan-outs — hunting latent bugs, drafting or judging plans, refuting claims, mapping a subsystem. Use it as agentType 'hardmode:scout' wherever a stage must read code and run checks but must never modify the working tree; the read-only hook enforces that deterministically. Not for implementing changes. ([blyatiful1/hardmode](https://github.com/blyatiful1/hardmode/blob/HEAD/agents/scout.md))
